# Skills · W · part 97

822 entries, writing-changelog to writing-durable-instructions. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **writing-changelog**: How to write a CHANGELOG.md entry - one bullet per change, one or two sentences, about what the user notices and never about how it was built. Load before adding or amending anything under Unreleased in CHANGELOG.md, before reviewing a diff that touches it, and before turning Unreleased into release notes. ([callstackincubator/appduct](https://github.com/callstackincubator/appduct/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/writing-changelog) · ★88)

- **writing-changelog**: Writes and audits entries in rw's CHANGELOG.md. Also use when deciding whether a change deserves an entry at all, when asked to shorten, clean up, or restructure the changelog, and when the user mentions release notes, the [Unreleased] section, or what to tell users about a change. ([rwdocs/rw](https://github.com/rwdocs/rw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/writing-changelog) · ★5)
